        generationSettings: currentGenerationSettings(),
        featureSettings: currentFeatureSettings(),
        sceneVisualControls: options['scene-visual-controls'] && !options['fixed-visual-controls'],
        sceneCountSelection,
        guernicaDirectory: usesGuernicaFigureReference() ? resolve(options['guernica-dir']) : null, results: [],
        imageSeed: runImageSeedBase === null ? null : (runImageSeedBase + imageSeedOffset) % 2_147_483_647,
      };
    }
    await mkdir(join(directory, 'media'), { recursive: true });
    // Old saved requests retain their original combined planning path on resume.
    if (!record.planningVersion && !record.plan && !await exists(join(directory, 'gpt-request.json'))) record.planningVersion = 2;
    const deferAutoSceneDurations = options['auto-mode'] && !Number.isInteger(record.sceneCount);
    if (!Object.hasOwn(record, 'sceneDurations') && !deferAutoSceneDurations) {
      record.sceneDurations = record.plan?.scenes?.map((scene) => scene.durationSeconds)
        || await sceneDurationStream.generate(record.sceneCount);
      record.sceneDurationSettings = sceneDurationStream.settings;
      await save(join(directory, 'record.json'), record);
    }
    const persist = async () => {
      await save(join(directory, 'record.json'), record);
      await writeFile(join(directory, 'index.html'), renderReport(record));
    };
    delete record.error;
    await persist();
    if (options['generate-title-cards']) {
      const iterationNumber = Number(basename(directory).match(/iteration-(\d+)/)?.[1]);
      record.titleCard = await renderTitleCard({
        runDirectory: iterationRoot,
        iterationDirectory: relative(iterationRoot, directory),
        iterationNumber,
      });
      await persist();
      console.log(`Title card ready: ${record.titleCard.articleTitle}`);
    }
    console.log(`Iteration: ${directory}\nArticle: ${record.article.title}`);
    if (!record.semantic) {
      record.semantic = await semanticSource.next();
      await save(join(directory, 'semantic-stream.json'), record.semantic);
      await persist();
      await semanticSource.acknowledge(record.semantic);
    }
    if (!record.articleImage) {
      record.articleImage = await download(record.article.image, directory, 'article');
      await persist();
    }
    await ensureAutoDecision(record, directory, openAiKey, persist, Boolean(resumeDirectory));
    if (!Number.isInteger(record.sceneCount) || record.sceneCount < 1 || record.sceneCount > 12) {
      throw new Error('Scene count must be selected and saved before story generation');
    }
    if (!Object.hasOwn(record, 'sceneDurations')) {
      record.sceneDurations = await sceneDurationStream.generate(record.sceneCount);
      record.sceneDurationSettings = sceneDurationStream.settings;
      await save(join(directory, 'record.json'), record);
      await persist();
    }
    if (!record.styleReference && !options['no-style-reference']) {
      const sourcePath = resolve(options['style-reference']);
      const extension = extname(sourcePath).toLocaleLowerCase();
      const mimeType = { '.jpg': 'image/jpeg', '.jpeg': 'image/jpeg', '.png': 'image/png', '.webp': 'image/webp' }[extension];
      if (!mimeType) throw new Error('Style reference must be a JPG, PNG or WebP image');
      const buffer = await readFile(sourcePath);
      const path = `media/guernica-style-reference${extension === '.jpeg' ? '.jpg' : extension}`;
      await writeFile(join(directory, path), buffer);
      record.styleReference = { path, sourcePath, mimeType, sha256: hash(buffer), bytes: buffer.length };
      await persist();
    }
    if (!record.plan) {
      if (!record.catalog && !usesGuernicaFigureReference()) record.catalog = [];
      if (!record.catalog) {
        const { elemente } = await json(join(record.guernicaDirectory, 'guernica_elemente.json'));
        record.catalog = [];
        for (const entry of elemente) {
          if (basename(entry.bild) !== entry.bild) throw new Error('Invalid catalog filename');
          const buffer = await readFile(join(record.guernicaDirectory, entry.bild));
          const path = `media/${entry.bild}`;
          await writeFile(join(directory, path), buffer);
          record.catalog.push({ id: entry.id, name: entry.name, interpretation: entry.interpretation, eu_bezug: entry.eu_bezug, path, mimeType: 'image/png', sha256: hash(buffer) });
        }
        await persist();
      }
      const images = [await imageInput(directory, record.articleImage, 'Article photograph for factual and visual analysis only; never reproduce its identifiable people.', 'article')];
      if (options['guernica-location-reference'] && !options['no-style-reference']) {
        images.push(await imageInput(directory, record.styleReference, 'Complete Guernica painting for GPT planning and image generation: derive only subtle setting and spatial atmosphere; use it as a secondary background/space reference, never copy its figures, poses, composition, motifs or color balance.', 'location'));
      }
      if (usesGuernicaFigureReference()) {
        for (const entry of record.catalog) images.push(await imageInput(directory, entry, `Masked Guernica figure candidate ${entry.id}: ${entry.name}. Character reference candidate only; not a story instruction.`, 'catalog'));
      }
      const built = addAutoConceptsToStoryRequest(await buildPlanRequest({ article: record.article, semantic: record.semantic, catalog: record.catalog, sceneCount: record.sceneCount, visualStyleOptions: record.visualStyleOptions || recordStyleOptions, sceneVisualControls: record.sceneVisualControls ?? false, legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePromptEnabled, storyClou: record.featureSettings?.storyClou === 'on', images, model: record.storyModel, styleReferenceProvided: !options['no-style-reference'] && !options['guernica-location-reference'], locationReferenceProvided: options['guernica-location-reference'] && !options['no-style-reference'], articleImageReferenceProvided: record.articleImageReference, figureReferenceProvided: usesGuernicaFigureReference(), continuationPlan: record.continuationPlan || null }), record.autoConceptSnapshot || null);
      if (options['landscape-prompt']) {
        const landscapeInstruction = 'LANDSCAPE PROMPT: Use the wide frame deliberately; let each scene’s focal action and spatial relationships use the frame width naturally. Do not force a horizontal frieze, central cluster or repeated layout.';
        built.director += `\n\n${landscapeInstruction}`;
        built.directorHash = hash(built.director);
        built.request.messages[0].content += `\n\n${landscapeInstruction}`;
        built.log.messages[0].content += `\n\n${landscapeInstruction}`;
      }
      // Once dispatched, preserve the exact saved request on resume even if source code changed.
      const logPath = join(directory, 'gpt-request.json');
      if (!await exists(logPath)) {
        record.directorHash = built.directorHash;
        await writeFile(join(directory, 'director.md'), built.director);
        await save(logPath, built.log);
        await persist();
      }
      const request = await json(logPath);
      for (const message of request.messages) {
        if (!Array.isArray(message.content)) continue;
        for (const part of message.content) {
          if (part.type !== 'image_url') continue;
          const image = images.find((candidate) => `local:${candidate.path}` === part.image_url.url);
          if (!image) throw new Error('Saved GPT request refers to an unknown image');
          part.image_url.url = image.dataUrl;
        }
      }
      const raw = await paidRequest(directory, 'gpt', 'https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions', openAiKey, request);
      if (raw.error) throw new Error(`GPT error: ${raw.error.message}`);
      let finalResponse = raw;
      try {
        record.semanticIndexCorrections = [];
        record.plan = validatePlan(raw, { ...record, visualOnly: record.planningVersion === 2, continuationPlan: record.continuationPlan || null, sceneVisualControls: record.sceneVisualControls ?? false, storyClou: record.featureSettings?.storyClou === 'on', figureReferenceProvided: record.guernicaFigureReference !== false });
      } catch (error) {
        if (!/semantic|motif|crocodile|protagonist|composition|figure|reference|visual control|story arc|clou|dramatic function/i.test(error.message)) throw error;
        const revisionRequest = {
          ...request,
          messages: [
            ...request.messages,
            { role: 'assistant', content: raw.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '' },
            { role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'text', text: [
              `Your plan failed validation: ${error.message}`,
              record.planningVersion === 2
                ? 'Revise the visual plan once. Do not assign durations or write video prompts. Every stillPrompt must be entirely natural English.'
                : 'Revise the whole plan once. Every stillPrompt and videoPrompt must be entirely natural English.',
              'Use the current stream fragments for free fictional associations before outlining the story. In semanticUse, relevance means creatively used, not factually related to the news. Let associations shape materials, gesture, mood, staging or story action. They may be surprising, indirect or playful; no factual, geographic or historical match is needed. Record your chosen motifs, scene indices and a short explanation in semanticUse; exact quotation or literal phrase repetition is not required. Keep the reported article facts distinct from the invented image story in fictionBoundary. Symbolism OFF permits stream-driven concrete fictional staging without allegorical overlays. Translate model prompts into natural English. Unused fragments are allowed; no minimum quota applies.',
              record.guernicaFigureReference === false
                ? `Keep one invented protagonist name and stable written visual identity for the entire scene sequence. Every protagonistAction must include the exact protagonist name. No Guernica figure or painting reference is supplied; set protagonist.guernicaId and every scene.guernicaId to 0. State in selectionReason that no Guernica figure reference is used. Derive identity, composition and gesture from the scene action, article and semantic association. ${record.articleImageReference ? 'The article photo is the only image reference and provides broad subject orientation only; do not copy its people, pose, framing, logos or text.' : 'No image reference is supplied; derive the protagonist and composition from the written scene plan.'}`
                : 'Keep one invented protagonist name and stable written visual identity for the entire scene sequence. Every protagonistAction must include the exact protagonist name. Choose a valid masked figure reference separately for each scene to match its action and body shape. References may recur, including in neighboring scenes, when appropriate; no variety quota applies. Every selectionReason must explain visual fit without importing the figure source story. Choose a supported composition from the scene action and current semantic association. Compositions may repeat; change camera angle, distance, framing and gesture only when the scene calls for it. The image model must ignore each reference crop’s exact pose, scale and placement.',
              record.sceneVisualControls ? 'For every scene, include its own visualStyleOptions object (symbolic boolean, roomDeepness boolean, layering integer 0–7) and visualControlReason of at least 20 characters. Decide values from that scene’s story beat, action and setting; do not repeat one run-wide setting without a scene-specific reason. The stillPrompt must implement those choices.' : '',
              'The crocodile is optional. Include it only if its action makes sense as a metaphor; never add it as an edge decoration.',
              record.guernicaFigureReference === false
                ? record.articleImageReference
                  ? 'The image API receives only reference image 1: the original article photograph, used for broad subject orientation only. No Guernica painting or masked figure crop is supplied. Never reproduce identifiable people, exact framing, logos, or text; redraw the underlying situation as the established fictional cubist mural.'
                  : 'The image API receives no reference images. No Guernica painting, figure crop, or article photograph is supplied. Derive the invented protagonist and composition from the written scene plan, with no copied visual reference.'
                : record.articleImageReference
                ? options['guernica-location-reference'] && !options['no-style-reference']
                  ? 'Each image API request receives three images: reference 1 is the selected masked figure, used only for the fictional protagonist’s abstract appearance and gesture; reference 2 is the complete Guernica painting, used only as a secondary setting and spatial-atmosphere reference; reference 3 is the original article photo, used only for broad visual orientation. Let the article-grounded action and protagonist lead. Use Guernica only for background space and atmosphere; do not reproduce its figures, pose, composition, motifs or color balance, or the photo’s identifiable people, exact composition, logos or text. Redraw the selected figure into the scene; never paste any reference. Do not import the figure’s original story, props or symbols. Keep protagonist prominent.'
                  : options['no-style-reference']
                    ? 'Each image API request receives two images: reference 1 is the selected masked figure, used only for the fictional protagonist’s abstract appearance and gesture; reference 2 is the original article photo, used only for broad visual orientation. Do not reproduce its identifiable people, exact composition, logos, or text. No complete painting reference is supplied; follow the written style description. Redraw the figure into the scene; never paste either reference. Do not import the figure’s original story, props, or symbols. Keep protagonist prominent.'
                    : 'Each image API request receives three images: reference 1 is the selected masked figure, used only for the fictional protagonist’s abstract appearance and gesture; reference 2 is the complete painting, used only for painterly style; reference 3 is the original article photo, used only for broad visual orientation. Do not reproduce the photo’s identifiable people, exact composition, logos, or text. Redraw the figure into the scene; never paste any reference. Do not import the figure’s original story, props, or symbols. Keep protagonist prominent.'
                : options['no-style-reference']
                  ? 'The image API receives only reference image 1: the selected masked figure, used only for the fictional protagonist’s abstract appearance and gesture. No complete painting reference is supplied; follow the written style description. Redraw reference 1 fully in the scene; never paste it. Do not import the figure’s original story, props, or symbols. The article photo is for GPT analysis only, never sent to the image API. Keep protagonist prominent. Do not copy real-person likenesses, crests, logos, brands, or readable lettering.'
                  : options['guernica-location-reference'] && !options['no-style-reference']
                  ? 'Each image API request receives two images: reference 1 is the selected masked figure, used only for the fictional protagonist’s abstract appearance and gesture; reference 2 is the complete Guernica painting, used only as a secondary setting and spatial-atmosphere reference. Let the article-grounded action and protagonist lead. Use Guernica only for background space and atmosphere; do not reproduce its figures, pose, composition, motifs or color balance. Make this new scene’s action and composition clearly distinct from Guernica. Redraw the selected figure into the scene; never paste it. Do not import its original story or props.'
                    : 'Each image API request receives two images: reference 1 is the selected masked figure, used only for the fictional protagonist’s abstract appearance and gesture; reference 2 is the complete painting, used only for style. Redraw reference 1 fully in the scene; never paste it. Do not import the figure’s original story, props, or symbols. Reference 2 contributes painterly style only. The article photo is for GPT analysis only, never sent to the image API. Keep protagonist prominent. Do not copy real-person likenesses, crests, logos, brands, or readable lettering.',
              'Here is the complete previous JSON plan to correct:',
              raw.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '',
            ].join('\n') }] },
          ],
        };
        const revisionLog = structuredClone(revisionRequest);
        let imageIndex = 0;
        for (const part of revisionLog.messages[1].content) {
          if (part.type === 'image_url') part.image_url.url = `local:${images[imageIndex++].path}`;
        }
        await save(join(directory, 'gpt-revision-request.json'), revisionLog);
        const revised = await paidRequest(directory, 'gpt-revision', 'https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions', openAiKey, revisionRequest);
        if (revised.error) throw new Error(`GPT revision failed: ${revised.error.message}`);
        record.semanticIndexCorrections = [];
        record.plan = validatePlan(revised, { ...record, visualOnly: record.planningVersion === 2, continuationPlan: record.continuationPlan || null, sceneVisualControls: record.sceneVisualControls ?? false, storyClou: record.featureSettings?.storyClou === 'on', figureReferenceProvided: record.guernicaFigureReference !== false });
        record.planRevisionReason = error.message;
        finalResponse = revised;
      }
      record.plan.scenes = applySceneVisualControls(record.plan.scenes, record.visualStyleOptions || recordStyleOptions);
      if (record.planningVersion === 2) {
        const visualPlan = visualPlanSnapshot(record.plan);
        record.visualPlanHash = hash(JSON.stringify(visualPlan));
        await save(join(directory, 'visual-plan.json'), visualPlan);
      } else {
        record.plan.scenes = record.plan.scenes.map((scene, index) => ({ ...scene, durationSeconds: record.sceneDurations ? record.sceneDurations[index] : scene.durationSeconds }));

          output = await download(result.imageUrl, directory, `scene-${scene.index}-first-frame`);
        } else {
          const raw = await paidRequest(directory, name, 'https://api.runware.ai/v1', runwareKey, [task]);
          if (raw.errors?.length) throw new Error(`Runware: ${raw.errors.map((error) => error.message || error.code).join('; ')}`);
          result = raw.data?.find((item) => item.taskUUID === task.taskUUID && item.imageURL);
          if (!result) throw new Error('Runware returned no matching image; saved response retained');
          output = await download(result.imageURL, directory, `scene-${scene.index}-first-frame`);
        }

'ZeroGPU quota/credits; exact GPU seconds not reported by Space' : 'provider-reported', promptHash: hash(scene.stillPrompt), referencePaths, referenceMetadata: [figureInput, locationInput, styleInput, articleInput].filter(Boolean).map(({ dataUrl, ...metadata }) => metadata) };
        await persist();
      }
    }
    let videoVision = null;
    if (record.featureSettings?.videoVision === 'on') {
      videoVision = await createVideoVision(record, directory, openAiKey, persist);
    }
    if (record.featureSettings?.videoDirection === 'on') {
      const beforeDirectionHash = hash(JSON.stringify(visualPlanSnapshot(record.plan)));
      await createVideoDirection(record, directory, openAiKey, persist, videoVision);
      if (hash(JSON.stringify(visualPlanSnapshot(record.plan))) !== beforeDirectionHash) throw new Error('Video direction changed the locked visual plan');
    }
    if (record.featureSettings?.videoAudioDirection === 'on') {
      await createVideoAudioDirection(record, directory, openAiKey, persist);
    }
    record.status = options['prompt-only'] ? 'Prompts erstellt' : 'Abgeschlossen';
    record.filmPlan = buildFilmPlan(record, directory);
    await save(join(directory, 'film-plan.json'), record.filmPlan);
    record.finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    await persist();
    await clearIterationLiveMarker(directory);
    liveMarkerWritten = false;
    console.log(`HTML: ${join(directory, 'index.html')}`);
    console.log(`Image cost USD: ${record.results.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item?.cost || 0), 0)}; GPT tokens: ${record.usage?.total_tokens}`);
    return directory;
  } catch (error) {
    if (record) {
      record.status = 'Unterbrochen'; record.error = redact(error.message);
      await save(join(directory, 'record.json'), record);
      await writeFile(join(directory, 'index.html'), renderReport(record));
    }
    if (liveMarkerWritten) {
      await writeIterationLiveMarker(directory, {
        ...liveMarker,
        status: 'interrupted',
        endedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        error: redact(error.message),
      }).catch((markerError) => console.error(`Could not update iteration live marker: ${markerError.message}`));
    }
    console.error(`Resume after resolving error: --resume=${directory}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function positiveInteger(value, fallback) {
  const number = Number(value ?? fallback);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(number) || number < 1) throw new Error(`Expected positive integer, got ${value}`);
  return number;
}

function log(message) {
  console.log(`[exhibition ${new Date().toISOString()}] ${message}`);
}

let wakeForStop;
const stopSignal = new Promise((resolveStop) => { wakeForStop = resolveStop; });
const delayOrStop = (milliseconds) => new Promise((resolveDelay) => {
  const timeout = setTimeout(resolveDelay, milliseconds);
  stopSignal.then(() => {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    resolveDelay();
  });
});
const readJson = async (path) => JSON.parse(await readFile(path, 'utf8'));

async function saveJson(path, value) {
  const temporaryPath = `${path}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  await writeFile(temporaryPath, `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`, 'utf8');
  await rename(temporaryPath, path);
}

async function listAggregateFiles() {
  let directoryEntries;
  try {
    directoryEntries = await readdir(newsDirectory);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return [];
    throw error;
  }
  return directoryEntries
    .filter((name) => /^all-.*\.json$/.test(name))
    .sort()
    .reverse();
}

function isNewsJsonFile(name) {
  return /^all-.*\.json$/.test(name)
    || /^eu-relevance-.*\.json$/.test(name)
    || /^[a-z]{2}-.*\.json$/.test(name);
}

function europeanTimestamp(date = new Date()) {
  const parts = Object.fromEntries(new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
    timeZone: 'Europe/Berlin', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit',
    hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit', hourCycle: 'h23',
  }).formatToParts(date).map(({ type, value }) => [type, value]));
  const milliseconds = String(date.getMilliseconds()).padStart(3, '0');
  return `${parts.year}-${parts.month}-${parts.day}T${parts.hour}-${parts.minute}-${parts.second}-${milliseconds}`;
}

async function archivePreviousNewsJsons(activeCollection) {
  const activeAggregateFile = basename(activeCollection.aggregatePath);
  const sourceTag = activeAggregateFile.replace(/^all-/, '').replace(/\.json$/, '');
  const activeFiles = new Set([
    activeAggregateFile,
    basename(activeCollection.relevancePath),
  ]);
  let entries;
  try {
    entries = await readdir(newsDirectory, { withFileTypes: true });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return;
    throw error;
  }
  const previousFiles = entries
    .filter((entry) => entry.isFile() && isNewsJsonFile(entry.name))
    .filter((entry) => !activeFiles.has(entry.name) && !entry.name.endsWith(`-${sourceTag}.json`))
    .map((entry) => entry.name);
  if (!previousFiles.length) return;

  const backupDirectory = join(newsDirectory, '_bak', europeanTimestamp());
  await mkdir(backupDirectory, { recursive: true });
  let archived = 0;
  for (const file of previousFiles) {
    try {
      await rename(join(newsDirectory, file), join(backupDirectory, file));
      archived += 1;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
    }
  }
  if (archived) log(`Archivierte ${archived} ältere News-/Ranking-JSONs nach ${backupDirectory}.`);
}

function runCommand(label, command, args, { onLine = null, inheritStdin = false } = {}) {
  return new Promise((resolveCommand, rejectCommand) => {
    const child = spawn(command, args, {
      cwd: repositoryRoot,
      env: process.env,
      stdio: [inheritStdin ? 'inherit' : 'ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    });
    children.add(child);
    const output = (stream, destination) => {
      const lines = createInterface({ input: stream, crlfDelay: Infinity });
      lines.on('line', (line) => {
        destination(line);
        onLine?.(line);
      });
    };
    output(child.stdout, (line) => console.log(line));
    output(child.stderr, (line) => console.error(line));
    child.once('error', (error) => {
      children.delete(child);
      rejectCommand(error);
    });
    child.once('close', (code, signal) => {
      children.delete(child);
      if (code === 0) resolveCommand({ code, signal });
      else rejectCommand(new Error(`${label} ended with ${signal || `exit ${code}`}`));
    });
  });
}

function flagName(argument) {
  return argument.startsWith('--') ? argument.split('=', 1)[0] : null;
}

function argumentValue(argumentsList, flag, fallback) {
  const index = argumentsList.findIndex((argument) => flagName(argument) === flag);
  if (index < 0) return fallback;
  const argument = argumentsList[index];
  return argument.includes('=') ? argument.slice(flag.length + 1) : argumentsList[index + 1] ?? fallback;
}

async function newestAggregate() {
  const files = await listAggregateFiles();
  if (!files.length) return null;
  const file = files[0];
  const path = join(newsDirectory, file);
  const aggregate = await readJson(path);
  return { file, path, aggregate };
}

async function refreshNewsIfNeeded() {
  const newest = await newestAggregate();
  const fetchedAtMs = newest ? Date.parse(newest.aggregate.fetchedAt || '') : NaN;
  const ageMs = Number.isFinite(fetchedAtMs) ? Date.now() - fetchedAtMs : Infinity;
  const rankedCollections = await findRankedNewsCollections(newsDirectory);
  const isFullyClassified = (collection) => collection
    && Number(collection.relevance.completedCount) >= Number(collection.relevance.articleCount)
    && Number(collection.relevance.failedCount || 0) === 0
    && Number(collection.relevance.unresolvedCount || 0) === 0;
  const latestIsRanked = rankedCollections[0]?.aggregateFile === newest?.file && isFullyClassified(rankedCollections[0]);

  if (!newest || ageMs >= refreshIntervalMs) {
    log(newest ? `News-Collection ist ${(ageMs / 3_600_000).toFixed(1)} h alt; Fetch startet.` : 'Keine News-Collection vorhanden; Fetch startet.');
    await runCommand('News-Fetch', process.execPath, [fetchScript]);
  }

  const afterFetch = await newestAggregate();
  const afterRanked = await findRankedNewsCollections(newsDirectory);
  const afterLatestIsRanked = afterRanked[0]?.aggregateFile === afterFetch?.file && isFullyClassified(afterRanked[0]);
  if (!afterLatestIsRanked) {
    log(`Jev bewertet neue Collection ${afterFetch?.file || '(fehlend)'}.`);
    await runCommand('Jev-EU-Bewertung', process.execPath, [classifyScript]);
  } else if (latestIsRanked && ageMs < refreshIntervalMs) {
    log(`News aktuell (${(ageMs / 3_600_000).toFixed(1)} h); Jev-Ranking vorhanden.`);
  }

  const completedRankings = await findRankedNewsCollections(newsDirectory);
  const completedLatest = completedRankings[0];
  if (completedLatest?.aggregateFile === afterFetch?.file && isFullyClassified(completedLatest)) {
    await archivePreviousNewsJsons(completedLatest);
  }
}

async function readState() {
  try { return await readJson(statePath); } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return {};
    throw error;
  }
}

async function rankedCollectionAvailable() {
  return (await findRankedNewsCollections(newsDirectory)).length > 0;
}

async function runRefreshLoop() {
  while (!stopping) {
    try {
      await refreshNewsIfNeeded();
      await delayOrStop(refreshCheckMs);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[exhibition] News refresh failed: ${error.message}`);
      await delayOrStop(refreshRetryMs);
    }
  }
}

async function getReadyIterations(runDirectory) {
  let manifest;
  try { manifest = await readJson(join(runDirectory, 'run.json')); } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return [];
    throw error;
  }
  const ready = [];
  for (const entry of manifest.iterations || []) {
    const iterationDirectory = join(runDirectory, entry.directory);
    const record = await readJson(join(iterationDirectory, 'record.json'));
    if (record.status !== 'Abgeschlossen') break;
    const scenes = record.plan?.scenes || [];
    if (scenes.length < 2 && !isNewAutoSingleScene(record)) break;
    const videos = await Promise.all(scenes.map(async (scene) => {
      try { await stat(join(iterationDirectory, 'media', `scene-${scene.index}-video.mp4`)); return true; }
      catch (error) { if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return false; throw error; }
    }));
    if (!isReadyForExhibition(record, videos)) break;
    ready.push(entry);
  }
  return ready;
}

async function updateFilms(runDirectory, readyIterations, videoSettings, generateTitleCards) {
  if (!glueEnabled || !readyIterations.length) return;
  const glueDirectory = join(runDirectory, 'glue-vids');
  const iterationCount = readyIterations.length;
  log(`Generate/resume First-Last-Frame bridges through iteration ${iterationCount}; wait source ${glueWaitPattern || 'Taktmuster 2/4/4 classic'}.`);
  await runCommand('First-Last-Frame-Glue', process.execPath, [
    glueScript,
    `--run=${runDirectory}`,
    `--iterations=${iterationCount}`,
    `--model=${videoSettings.model}`,
    `--resolution=${videoSettings.resolution}`,
    `--settings-mode=${videoSettings.settingsMode}`,
    `--duration-pattern=${gluePattern}`,
    `--transition-wait-pattern=${glueWaitPattern}`,
    `--frame-offset-seconds=${glueFrameOffset}`,
    `--output-dir=${glueDirectory}`,
  ]);

  const normalizedIterationDirectory = join(glueDirectory, 'normalized-iterations');
  const normalizeAudioScript = join(scriptDirectory, 'normalize-iteration-audio.mjs');
  const stableSeed = createHash('sha256').update(runDirectory).digest('hex').slice(0, 16);
  const iterationFilmVariants = [
    { name: 'full', bridgeArguments: [] },
    {
      name: 'random-bridges',
      bridgeArguments: [`--bridge-random-threshold=${randomThreshold}`, `--bridge-random-seed=${stableSeed}`],
    },
  ];
  for (const variant of iterationFilmVariants) {
    for (const entry of readyIterations) {
      const record = await readJson(join(runDirectory, entry.directory, 'record.json'));
      const variantDirectory = join(normalizedIterationDirectory, variant.name);
      const intermediatePath = join(variantDirectory, `${entry.directory}-before-audio-normalization.mp4`);
      const normalizedPath = join(variantDirectory, `${entry.directory}-audio-normalized.mp4`);
      await runCommand(`Assemble ${entry.directory} (${variant.name})`, process.execPath, [
        assembleScript,
        `--run-dir=${runDirectory}`,
        `--iteration=${entry.directory}`,
        `--scene-glue-dir=${glueDirectory}`,
        '--existing-bridges-only',
        `--frame-offset-seconds=${glueFrameOffset}`,
        `--fade-seconds=${fadeSeconds}`,
        ...(generateTitleCards ? ['--generate-title-cards'] : ['--no-generate-title-cards']),
        ...variant.bridgeArguments,
        `--output=${intermediatePath}`,
      ]);
      await runCommand(`Normalize ${entry.directory} audio (${variant.name})`, process.execPath, [
        normalizeAudioScript,
        `--input=${intermediatePath}`,
        `--output=${normalizedPath}`,
      ]);
    }
  }

  const baseArguments = [
    assembleScript,
    `--run-dir=${runDirectory}`,
    `--iteration-film-dir=${join(normalizedIterationDirectory, 'full')}`,
    `--limit=${iterationCount}`,
    '--no-generate-title-cards',
    `--fade-seconds=${fadeSeconds}`,
  ];
  await runCommand('Full exhibition film', process.execPath, [
    ...baseArguments,
    `--output=${join(glueDirectory, 'eu-reski-live-full.mp4')}`,
  ]);
  await runCommand('Sparse random-bridge film', process.execPath, [
    assembleScript,
    `--run-dir=${runDirectory}`,
    `--iteration-film-dir=${join(normalizedIterationDirectory, 'random-bridges')}`,
    `--limit=${iterationCount}`,
    '--no-generate-title-cards',
    `--fade-seconds=${fadeSeconds}`,
    `--output=${join(glueDirectory, 'eu-reski-live-random-bridges.mp4')}`,
  ]);

  const runManifestPath = join(runDirectory, 'run.json');
  const runManifest = await readJson(runManifestPath);
  const glueSummary = await readJson(join(glueDirectory, 'glue-run.json'));
  runManifest.assembledFilm = {
    status: 'ready',
    path: 'glue-vids/eu-reski-live-full.mp4',
    iterations: iterationCount,
    transitionCount: glueSummary.transitionCount,
    transitionWaitPattern: glueSummary.transitionWaitPattern,
    method: `First/Last-Frame-Glue · ${glueSummary.transitionCount} Übergänge · ${glueSummary.transitionWaitPattern} · Audio je Iteration normalisiert`,
    audioNormalization: {
      method: 'FFmpeg loudnorm after assembling each iteration with its bridges',
      targetIntegratedLufs: -16,
      maximumTruePeakDbtp: -1.5,
      targetLoudnessRangeLu: 11,
      iterationFilmsDirectory: 'glue-vids/normalized-iterations',
    },
  };
  await saveJson(runManifestPath, runManifest);
  const reportIterations = await Promise.all((runManifest.iterations || []).map(async (entry) => ({
    directory: entry.directory,
    record: await readJson(join(runDirectory, entry.directory, 'record.json')),
  })));
  await writeFile(join(runDirectory, 'index.html'), renderRunReport(runManifest, reportIterations));

  const state = await readState();
  state.runDirectory = runDirectory;
  state.gluedThroughRunDirectory = runDirectory;
  state.gluedThroughIteration = iterationCount;
  state.lastFilmBuildAt = new Date().toISOString();
  await saveJson(statePath, state);
}

async function runMergeLoop(runDirectory, videoSettings, generateTitleCards) {
  while (!stopping) {
    if (mergeRunning) {
      await delayOrStop(mergeCheckMs);
      continue;
    }
    mergeRunning = true;
    try {
      const ready = await getReadyIterations(runDirectory);
      const state = await readState();
      const gluedThroughIteration = state.gluedThroughRunDirectory === runDirectory
        ? Number(state.gluedThroughIteration || 0)
        : 0;
      if (ready.length > gluedThroughIteration) await updateFilms(runDirectory, ready, videoSettings, generateTitleCards);
      await delayOrStop(mergeCheckMs);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[exhibition] Film merge failed: ${error.message}`);
      await delayOrStop(mergeRetryMs);
    } finally {
      mergeRunning = false;
    }
  }
}

let stopping = false;
let runnerChild = null;
let mergeRunning = false;
let state = {};
const children = new Set();

function requestStop(signal) {
  if (stopping) return;
  stopping = true;
  wakeForStop();
  log(`${signal} received; stopping child processes.`);
  for (const child of children) child.kill(signal);
  runnerChild?.kill(signal);
}

process.once('SIGINT', () => requestStop('SIGINT'));
process.once('SIGTERM', () => requestStop('SIGTERM'));

async function withLock(task) {
  await mkdir(outputDirectory, { recursive: true });
  let lockHandle;
  try {
    lockHandle = await open(lockPath, 'wx');
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'EEXIST') {
      const owner = Number((await readFile(lockPath, 'utf8').catch(() => '')).trim());
      let processIsAlive = Number.isInteger(owner) && owner > 0;
      if (processIsAlive) {
        try { process.kill(owner, 0); } catch (ownerError) { if (ownerError.code === 'ESRCH') processIsAlive = false; }
      }
      if (!processIsAlive) {
        await unlink(lockPath).catch(() => {});
        lockHandle = await open(lockPath, 'wx');
      } else throw new Error(`Another exhibition runner (PID ${owner}) owns ${lockPath}`);
    } else {
      throw error;
    }
  }
  await lockHandle.writeFile(`${process.pid}\n`);
  try { await task(); } finally {
    await lockHandle.close();
    const currentLock = await readFile(lockPath, 'utf8').catch(() => '');
    if (currentLock.trim() === String(process.pid)) await unlink(lockPath);
  }
}

function startRunner(argumentsList, runDirectory, onRunDirectory) {
  let resolveClosed;
  let rejectClosed;
  const closed = new Promise((resolveRunner, rejectRunner) => {
    resolveClosed = resolveRunner;
    rejectClosed = rejectRunner;
  });
  try {
    let globalArticleQueueExhausted = false;
    const args = [runnerScript, ...argumentsList];
    if (runDirectory && !args.some((argument) => flagName(argument) === '--resume-run')) args.push(`--resume-run=${runDirectory}`);
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, args, { cwd: repositoryRoot, env: process.env, stdio: ['inherit', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
    runnerChild = child;
    children.add(child);
    const onLine = (line) => {
      console.log(line);
      const match = /^Run folder: (.+)$/.exec(line);
      if (!match) return;
      state.runDirectory = match[1];
      saveJson(statePath, state).catch((error) => console.error(`[exhibition] Could not save run pointer: ${error.message}`));
      onRunDirectory?.(match[1]);
    };
    const stdoutLines = createInterface({ input: child.stdout, crlfDelay: Infinity });
    const stderrLines = createInterface({ input: child.stderr, crlfDelay: Infinity });
    stdoutLines.on('line', onLine);
    stderrLines.on('line', (line) => {
      console.error(line);
      if (line.includes('Global article queue exhausted:')) globalArticleQueueExhausted = true;
    });
    child.once('error', (error) => {
      children.delete(child);
      runnerChild = null;
      rejectClosed(error);
    });
    child.once('close', (code, signal) => {
      children.delete(child);
      runnerChild = null;
      resolveClosed({ code, signal, globalArticleQueueExhausted });
    });
    return { child, closed };
  } catch (error) {
    rejectClosed(error);
    return { child: null, closed };
  }
}

async function exhibitionMain() {
  if (process.argv.includes('--help')) {
    console.log('Start exhibition run. News refreshes every 24 h; Jev ranks EU relevance; linear article order.');
    console.log('Use --stop-after-iterations=N for a finite run; final First-Last glue and film merges finish before exit.');
    console.log('Flags: --no-first-last-glue disables generated bridges; bridges are on by default.');
    console.log('Flags: --auto-mode enables per-iteration artistic direction; --no-auto-mode disables it.');
    console.log('Environment: NEWS_ENV_FILE (in start script), NEWS_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS.');
    return;
  }
  await withLock(async () => {
    state = await readState();
    const commandArguments = process.argv.slice(2);
    const resumeArgument = commandArguments.find((argument) => flagName(argument) === '--resume-run');
    const resumePath = resumeArgument ? argumentValue(commandArguments, '--resume-run', '') : '';
    if (resumeArgument && (!resumePath || resumePath.startsWith('--'))) throw new Error('Use --resume-run=PATH');
    if (resumePath) state.runDirectory = resolve(resumePath);
    const priorRun = state.runDirectory
      ? await readJson(join(state.runDirectory, 'run.json')).catch(() => null)
      : null;
    if (resumePath && !priorRun) throw new Error(`Cannot resume: no run.json in ${state.runDirectory}`);
    const priorRunIsIncomplete = priorRun && (Boolean(resumePath) || ['running', 'pending_media'].includes(priorRun.status));
    if (state.runDirectory && !priorRunIsIncomplete) {
      state.runDirectory = null;
      delete state.scenePath;
      delete state.scenePaths;
      delete state.scenePlanFingerprint;
    }
    log(`News directory: ${newsDirectory}; output directory: ${outputDirectory}`);
    if (state.settingsFingerprint !== settingsFingerprint) {
      const savedRun = state.runDirectory
        ? await readJson(join(state.runDirectory, 'run.json')).catch(() => null)
        : null;
      const resumableRun = savedRun && (Boolean(resumePath) || ['running', 'pending_media'].includes(savedRun.status));
      if (resumableRun) {
        log(`Defaults changed, but run ${state.runDirectory} is incomplete; preserving its saved arguments and run snapshot for resume.`);
      } else {
        if (state.runDirectory) log(`Default settings changed; new run will use ${settingsPath} version ${defaultSettings.version}. Existing run files remain intact.`);
        state.runnerArguments = null;
        state.runDirectory = null;
        state.storyModel = null;
        state.settingsSource = null;
        state.exhibitionSettings = null;
      }
    }
    const exhibitionArguments = commandArguments;
    const sceneArgumentValues = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < exhibitionArguments.length; index += 1) {
      if (flagName(exhibitionArguments[index]) !== '--scene') continue;
      const value = exhibitionArguments[index].includes('=')
        ? exhibitionArguments[index].slice('--scene='.length)
        : exhibitionArguments[++index];
      if (!value || value.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Use --scene=PATH; repeat --scene=PATH to schedule several scene files');
      sceneArgumentValues.push(value);
    }
    const scenePaths = sceneArgumentValues.map((scenePath) => resolve(scenePath));
    const hasSceneArgument = scenePaths.length > 0;
    const generationIntervalArgument = exhibitionArguments.findLast((argument) => flagName(argument) === '--generation-interval');
    if (generationIntervalArgument && !hasSceneArgument) {
      throw new Error('--generation-interval requires --scene=PATH');
    }
    const generationInterval = generationIntervalArgument
      ? generationIntervalArgument.includes('=')
        ? generationIntervalArgument.slice('--generation-interval='.length)
        : exhibitionArguments[exhibitionArguments.indexOf(generationIntervalArgument) + 1]
      : undefined;
    if (generationIntervalArgument) {
      if (!generationInterval || generationInterval.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Use --generation-interval=1h');
    }
    const loadedSceneSequences = scenePaths.length
      ? await Promise.all(scenePaths.map((scenePath) => loadSceneSequence(scenePath, defaultGeneration, {
        scheduleEnabled: Boolean(generationIntervalArgument),
        generationInterval,
      })))
      : null;
    if (loadedSceneSequences?.length > 1) {
      const firstSequence = loadedSceneSequences[0];
      for (const sequence of loadedSceneSequences.slice(1)) {
        if (sequence.scheduleEnabled !== firstSequence.scheduleEnabled
          || sequence.intervalMs !== firstSequence.intervalMs
          || sequence.biasMode !== firstSequence.biasMode) {
          throw new Error('Scheduled scene files must use the same interval and bias mode; set --generation-interval=... to share one interval.');
        }
      }
    }
    const sceneSequence = loadedSceneSequences?.length ? {
      path: scenePaths.length === 1 ? scenePaths[0] : scenePaths.join(' | '),
      paths: scenePaths,
      scheduleEnabled: loadedSceneSequences[0].scheduleEnabled,
      generationInterval: loadedSceneSequences[0].generationInterval,
      intervalMs: loadedSceneSequences[0].intervalMs,
      biasMode: loadedSceneSequences[0].biasMode,
      iterators: loadedSceneSequences.flatMap((sequence, sequenceIndex) => sequence.iterators.map((iterator) => ({
        ...iterator,
        name: loadedSceneSequences.length === 1 ? iterator.name : `${basename(scenePaths[sequenceIndex])} · ${iterator.name}`,
      }))),
    } : null;
    const activeScenePaths = state.scenePaths || (state.scenePath ? [state.scenePath] : []);
    if (state.runDirectory && activeScenePaths.length && !sceneSequence) {
      throw new Error(`Resume the active scene sequence with ${activeScenePaths.map((scenePath) => `--scene=${scenePath}`).join(' ')}`);
    }
    if (state.runDirectory && activeScenePaths.length
      && JSON.stringify(sceneSequence?.paths || [sceneSequence?.path]) !== JSON.stringify(activeScenePaths)) {
      throw new Error(`Active scene sequence uses ${activeScenePaths.join(', ')}; resume with the same --scene files before selecting another sequence`);
    }
    const stopAfterArgument = exhibitionArguments.find((argument) => flagName(argument) === '--stop-after-iterations');
    let stopAfterIterations = null;
    if (stopAfterArgument) {
      const value = stopAfterArgument.includes('=')
        ? stopAfterArgument.slice('--stop-after-iterations='.length)
        : exhibitionArguments[exhibitionArguments.indexOf(stopAfterArgument) + 1];
      stopAfterIterations = Number(value);
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(stopAfterIterations) || stopAfterIterations < 1) {
        throw new Error('--stop-after-iterations must be a positive integer');
      }
    }
    if (exhibitionArguments.includes('--first-last-glue') && exhibitionArguments.includes('--no-first-last-glue')) {
      throw new Error('Choose either --first-last-glue or --no-first-last-glue');
    }
    if (exhibitionArguments.includes('--auto-mode') && exhibitionArguments.includes('--no-auto-mode')) {
      throw new Error('Choose either --auto-mode or --no-auto-mode');
    }
    if (exhibitionArguments.includes('--first-last-glue')) glueEnabled = true;
    if (exhibitionArguments.includes('--no-first-last-glue')) glueEnabled = false;
    if (!exhibitionArguments.includes('--first-last-glue') && !exhibitionArguments.includes('--no-first-last-glue')
      && process.env.EXHIBITION_FIRST_LAST_GLUE === undefined && state.exhibitionSettings) {
      glueEnabled = state.exhibitionSettings.glueEnabled;
    }
    if (state.exhibitionSettings) {
      if (process.env.EXHIBITION_GLUE_DURATION_PATTERN === undefined) gluePattern = state.exhibitionSettings.gluePattern;
      if (process.env.EXHIBITION_GLUE_WAIT_PATTERN === undefined) glueWaitPattern = state.exhibitionSettings.glueWaitPattern || glueWaitPattern;
      if (process.env.EXHIBITION_FRAME_OFFSET_SECONDS === undefined) glueFrameOffset = state.exhibitionSettings.glueFrameOffset;
      if (process.env.EXHIBITION_FADE_SECONDS === undefined) fadeSeconds = state.exhibitionSettings.fadeSeconds;
      if (process.env.EXHIBITION_RANDOM_BRIDGE_THRESHOLD === undefined) randomThreshold = state.exhibitionSettings.randomThreshold;
    }
    const requestedTransitionWaitPattern = argumentValue(exhibitionArguments, '--transition-wait-pattern', undefined);
    if (requestedTransitionWaitPattern !== undefined) glueWaitPattern = requestedTransitionWaitPattern;
    if (!(randomThreshold >= 0 && randomThreshold < 1)) throw new Error('EXHIBITION_RANDOM_BRIDGE_THRESHOLD must be from 0 to below 1');
    const glueDurations = gluePattern.split(',').map((value) => Number(value.trim()));
    if (!glueDurations.length || glueDurations.some((value) => !Number.isInteger(value) || value < 5 || value > 15)) {
      throw new Error('EXHIBITION_GLUE_DURATION_PATTERN must contain integer seconds from 5 through 15');
    }
    const frameOffset = Number(glueFrameOffset);
    if (!Number.isFinite(frameOffset) || frameOffset <= 0 || frameOffset > 1) throw new Error('EXHIBITION_FRAME_OFFSET_SECONDS must be greater than 0 and no more than 1');
    const fadeLength = Number(fadeSeconds);
    if (!Number.isFinite(fadeLength) || fadeLength < 0 || fadeLength > 2) throw new Error('EXHIBITION_FADE_SECONDS must be from 0 through 2');
    const userArguments = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < exhibitionArguments.length; index += 1) {
      const argument = exhibitionArguments[index];
      if (['--first-last-glue', '--no-first-last-glue', '--stop-after-iterations', '--transition-wait-pattern', '--scene', '--generation-interval', '--resume-run'].includes(flagName(argument))) {
        if (['--stop-after-iterations', '--transition-wait-pattern', '--scene', '--generation-interval', '--resume-run'].includes(flagName(argument)) && !argument.includes('=')) index += 1;
        continue;
      }
      userArguments.push(argument);
    }
    let defaults;
    if (sceneSequence) {
      defaults = defaultsFromExhibitionSettings(sceneSequence.iterators[0].generation);
    } else if (state.runnerArguments?.length) {
      defaults = { args: state.runnerArguments, storyModel: state.storyModel, source: state.settingsSource || null };
    } else {
      defaults = defaultsFromExhibitionSettings();
      state.runnerArguments = defaults.args;
      state.storyModel = defaults.storyModel;
      state.settingsSource = defaults.source;
    }
    const requestedStoryModel = argumentValue(
      userArguments,
      '--story-model',
      process.env.NEWS_STORY_MODEL?.trim() || '',
    ).trim();
    if (requestedStoryModel) defaults = { ...defaults, storyModel: requestedStoryModel };
    if (stopAfterIterations === null && !userArguments.some((argument) => flagName(argument) === '--iterations')) {
      const configuredIterations = Number(argumentValue(defaults.args, '--iterations', '-1'));
      if (Number.isSafeInteger(configuredIterations) && configuredIterations > 0) {
        stopAfterIterations = configuredIterations;
      }
    }
    if (stopAfterIterations !== null) {
      for (let index = userArguments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
        if (flagName(userArguments[index]) === '--iterations') userArguments.splice(index, 1);
      }
      userArguments.push(`--iterations=${stopAfterIterations}`);
    }
    const configuredAutoMode = defaults.args.includes('--no-auto-mode') ? false
      : defaults.args.includes('--auto-mode') ? true : defaultGeneration.autoMode === true;
    const autoModeEnabled = resolveAutoModeSetting(configuredAutoMode, exhibitionArguments);
    const iteratorPlans = sceneSequence
      ? sceneSequence.iterators.map((iterator) => {
        const iteratorDefaults = defaultsFromExhibitionSettings(iterator.generation);
        let iteratorArguments = mergeRunnerArguments(iteratorDefaults.args, userArguments, { stopAfterIterations, newsDirectory, outputDirectory });
        if (sceneSequence.iterators.length > 1) {
          if (iteratorArguments.some((argument) => flagName(argument) === '--article-id')) {
            throw new Error('Scheduled scene files cannot pin --article-id; global article order prevents repeats.');
          }
          iteratorArguments = iteratorArguments.filter((argument) => flagName(argument) !== '--article-order');
          iteratorArguments.push('--article-order=global');
        }
        return { name: iterator.name, arguments: iteratorArguments };
      })
      : null;
    const runnerArguments = iteratorPlans?.[0].arguments
      || mergeRunnerArguments(defaults.args, userArguments, { stopAfterIterations, newsDirectory, outputDirectory });
    const normalizedRunnerArguments = runnerArguments.filter((argument) => !['--auto-mode', '--no-auto-mode'].includes(flagName(argument)));
    if (autoModeEnabled) normalizedRunnerArguments.push('--auto-mode');
    else if (defaultGeneration.autoMode === true || defaults.args.includes('--no-auto-mode')
      || exhibitionArguments.includes('--no-auto-mode')) normalizedRunnerArguments.push('--no-auto-mode');
    let iteratorPlanPath = null;
    if (iteratorPlans) {
      iteratorPlanPath = join(outputDirectory, '.exhibition-iterator-plan.json');
      const iteratorPlan = {
        scenePath: sceneSequence.path,
        scheduleEnabled: sceneSequence.scheduleEnabled,
        generationInterval: sceneSequence.generationInterval,
        intervalMs: sceneSequence.intervalMs,
        biasMode: sceneSequence.biasMode,
        iterators: iteratorPlans,
      };
      if (priorRunIsIncomplete && state.runDirectory && priorRun?.iteratorPlanFingerprint) {
        const savedPlan = await readJson(iteratorPlanPath);
        const comparable = (plan) => ({
          ...plan,
          iterators: plan.iterators.map((iterator) => ({
            ...iterator,
            arguments: iterator.arguments.filter((argument) => !['--iterations', '--article-order'].includes(flagName(argument))),
          })),
        });
        if (JSON.stringify(comparable(savedPlan)) !== JSON.stringify(comparable(iteratorPlan))) {
          throw new Error('Scene settings changed; resume requires the original scene plan.');
        }
        const savedFingerprint = createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(savedPlan)).digest('hex');
        if (savedFingerprint !== priorRun.iteratorPlanFingerprint) throw new Error('Saved scene plan does not match the interrupted run.');
        log('Resuming the saved scene plan; iteration limit and global article order may be changed explicitly.');
      } else {
        await saveJson(iteratorPlanPath, iteratorPlan);
      }
      state.scenePath = sceneSequence.path;
      state.scenePaths = scenePaths;
      state.scenePlanFingerprint = createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(await readJson(iteratorPlanPath))).digest('hex');
      normalizedRunnerArguments.push(`--iterator-plan=${iteratorPlanPath}`);
    } else {
      delete state.scenePath;
      delete state.scenePaths;
      delete state.scenePlanFingerprint;
    }
    const videoSettings = {
      model: argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--video-model', defaultGeneration.videoModel),
      resolution: argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--video-resolution', defaultGeneration.videoResolution),
      settingsMode: argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--video-settings-mode', defaultGeneration.videoSettingsMode),
    };
    log(`Settings source: ${defaults.source || 'exhibition defaults'}`);
    log(`Settings snapshot: ${JSON.stringify({
      storyModel: defaults.storyModel,
      sceneCount: {
        mode: argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--scene-count-mode', 'fixed'),
        takt: argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--scene-count-takt', '2'),
        meter: `${argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--scene-count-numerator', '3')}/${argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--scene-count-denominator', '4')}`,
        type: argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--scene-count-type', 'balanced'),
        bias: argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--scene-count-bias', '2'),
        fixedCount: argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--scenes', null),
      },
      articleOrder: argumentValue(runnerArguments, '--article-order', 'linear'),
      sceneVideo: { enabled: runnerArguments.includes('--videos-after-each-iteration'), ...videoSettings },
      visualControlMode: runnerArguments.includes('--legacy-guernica-style-reference-prompt')
        && !runnerArguments.includes('--no-style-reference')
        && !runnerArguments.includes('--article-image-only-reference')
        && !runnerArguments.includes('--guernica-location-reference')
        ? 'fixed'
        : runnerArguments.includes('--scene-visual-controls') ? 'scene-specific' : 'fixed',
      generateTitleCards: runnerArguments.includes('--generate-title-cards'),
      firstLastGlue: glueEnabled,
      gluePattern,
      glueWaitPattern,
      frameOffsetSeconds: glueFrameOffset,
      fadeSeconds,
      randomBridgeThreshold: randomThreshold,
    })}`);
    state.runnerArguments = runnerArguments
      .filter((argument) => flagName(argument) !== '--resume-run')
      .map((argument) => stopAfterIterations !== null && flagName(argument) === '--iterations' ? '--iterations=-1' : argument);
    for (let index = state.runnerArguments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
      if (flagName(state.runnerArguments[index]) === '--auto-mode' || flagName(state.runnerArguments[index]) === '--no-auto-mode') {
        state.runnerArguments.splice(index, 1);
      }
    }
    if (autoModeEnabled) state.runnerArguments.push('--auto-mode');
    else if (defaultGeneration.autoMode === true || defaults.args.includes('--no-auto-mode')
      || exhibitionArguments.includes('--no-auto-mode')) state.runnerArguments.push('--no-auto-mode');
    state.storyModel = defaults.storyModel;
    state.settingsSource = defaults.source;
    state.settingsVersion = defaultSettings.version;
    state.settingsFingerprint = settingsFingerprint;
    state.exhibitionSettings = { glueEnabled, gluePattern, glueWaitPattern, glueFrameOffset, fadeSeconds, randomThreshold };
    state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    await saveJson(statePath, state);
    if (state.storyModel) process.env.NEWS_STORY_MODEL = state.storyModel;

    let runDirectory = state.runDirectory ? resolve(state.runDirectory) : null;
    if (!runDirectory || !(await stat(join(runDirectory, 'run.json')).catch(() => null))) runDirectory = null;
    if (stopAfterIterations !== null && !runDirectory) {
      state.runDirectory = null;
      state.gluedThroughIteration = 0;
      delete state.lastFilmBuildAt;
      await saveJson(statePath, state);
    }

    if (!await rankedCollectionAvailable()) {
      log('Noch kein Jev-Ranking vorhanden; initialer News-Refresh läuft vor der ersten Iteration.');
      await refreshNewsIfNeeded();
    }
    if (!await rankedCollectionAvailable()) throw new Error(`No Jev-ranked collection in ${newsDirectory}`);

    const refreshTask = runRefreshLoop();
    const generateTitleCards = runnerArguments.includes('--generate-title-cards');
    let mergeTask = runDirectory ? runMergeLoop(runDirectory, videoSettings, generateTitleCards) : null;
    while (!stopping) {
      const running = startRunner(normalizedRunnerArguments, runDirectory, (reportedRunDirectory) => {
        runDirectory = resolve(reportedRunDirectory);
        if (!mergeTask) mergeTask = runMergeLoop(runDirectory, videoSettings, generateTitleCards);
      });
      const result = await running.closed;
      if (state.runDirectory) runDirectory = resolve(state.runDirectory);
      if (stopping) break;
      if (stopAfterIterations !== null && result.code === 0) {
        const savedRun = runDirectory ? await readJson(join(runDirectory, 'run.json')).catch(() => null) : null;
        if (savedRun?.status === 'pending_media') {
          stopping = true;
          wakeForStop();
          log(`Run paused with pending media (${(savedRun.pendingMedia || []).length} iteration(s)); restart runner to poll saved provider task IDs.`);
        } else {
          if (glueEnabled && runDirectory) {
            log(`Iteration limit ${stopAfterIterations} reached; waiting for First-Last glue and final films.`);
            while (!stopping) {
              const latestState = await readState();
              if (resolve(latestState.runDirectory || '') === runDirectory
                && Number(latestState.gluedThroughIteration || 0) >= stopAfterIterations) break;
              await delayOrStop(mergeCheckMs);
            }
          }
          if (!stopping) {
            stopping = true;
            wakeForStop();
            log(`Finite exhibition complete after ${stopAfterIterations} iterations; no next iteration started.`);
            for (const child of children) child.kill('SIGTERM');
          }
        }
        break;
      }
      if (result.globalArticleQueueExhausted) {
        log(`All eligible articles used; keep scene schedule paused until news refresh. Check again in ${refreshCheckMs} ms.`);
        await delayOrStop(refreshCheckMs);
      } else {
        log(`Image runner exited (${result.signal || `code ${result.code}`}); resume same run in 30 s.`);
        await delayOrStop(30_000);
      }
    }
    await Promise.allSettled([refreshTask, mergeTask].filter(Boolean));
  });
}

exhibitionMain().catch((error) => {
  console.error(`[exhibition] ${error.stack || error.message}`);
  process.exitCode = 1;
});

import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdir, readFile, readdir, rename, stat, unlink, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { parseArgs } from 'node:util';
import { isRetryableHttpStatus, isRetryableNetworkError, retryDelayMs, retryTransient } from '../news-scene/provider-resilience.mjs';
import { createTransitionWaitScheduler, parseTransitionWaitPattern } from '../news-scene/transition-wait-pattern.mjs';

const { values: options } = parseArgs({ options: {
  run: { type: 'string' }, iterations: { type: 'string', default: '5' },
  'output-dir': { type: 'string' }, model: { type: 'string', default: 'prunaai:p-video@2-pro' },
  resolution: { type: 'string', default: '480p' }, 'settings-mode': { type: 'string', default: 'cost' },
  duration: { type: 'string', default: '5' }, 'duration-pattern': { type: 'string' },
  'transition-wait-pattern': { type: 'string' },
  'frame-offset-seconds': { type: 'string', default: '0.25' }, concurrency: { type: 'string', default: '4' },
  'poll-timeout-seconds': { type: 'string', default: '600' }, help: { type: 'boolean', default: false },
} });

if (options.help) {
  console.log('node --env-file=.env eu-reski-news/scripts/generate-scene-glue-videos.mjs --run=PATH [--iterations=5] [--transition-wait-pattern=LIST|all] [--output-dir=PATH]');
  process.exit(0);
}

if (!options.run) throw new Error('--run=PATH is required');
if (options.model !== 'prunaai:p-video@2-pro') throw new Error('Only prunaai:p-video@2-pro supports this first/last-frame workflow');
if (options.resolution !== '480p') throw new Error('--resolution must be 480p to match the source clips');
if (options['settings-mode'] !== 'cost') throw new Error('--settings-mode must be cost');

const iterationLimit = Number(options.iterations);
const duration = Number(options.duration);
const frameOffsetSeconds = Number(options['frame-offset-seconds']);
const durationPattern = options['duration-pattern']
  ? options['duration-pattern'].split(',').map((value) => Number(value.trim()))
  : null;
const durationTaktmuster = durationPattern ? {
  index: 0,
  getNext() {
    const patternIndex = this.index;
    this.index += 1;
    return { patternValue: durationPattern[patternIndex % durationPattern.length], patternIndex };
  },
  } : null;
const transitionWaitPattern = parseTransitionWaitPattern(options['transition-wait-pattern']);
const concurrency = Number(options.concurrency);
const pollTimeoutSeconds = Number(options['poll-timeout-seconds']);
if (!Number.isInteger(iterationLimit) || iterationLimit < 1) throw new Error('--iterations must be a positive integer');
if (!Number.isInteger(duration) || duration < 5 || duration > 15) throw new Error('--duration must be an integer from 5 to 15');
if (!Number.isFinite(frameOffsetSeconds) || frameOffsetSeconds <= 0 || frameOffsetSeconds > 1) {
  throw new Error('--frame-offset-seconds must be greater than 0 and no more than 1');
}
if (durationPattern && (!durationPattern.length || durationPattern.some((value) => !Number.isInteger(value) || value < 5 || value > 15))) {
  throw new Error('--duration-pattern must be comma-separated integer durations from 5 to 15 seconds');
}
if (!Number.isInteger(concurrency) || concurrency < 1 || concurrency > 8) throw new Error('--concurrency must be an integer from 1 to 8');
if (!Number.isInteger(pollTimeoutSeconds) || pollTimeoutSeconds < 10 || pollTimeoutSeconds > 1800) {
  throw new Error('--poll-timeout-seconds must be an integer from 10 to 1800');
}

const apiKey = process.env.RUNWARE_API_KEY || process.env.RUNWARE_KEY;
if (!apiKey) throw new Error('Set RUNWARE_API_KEY or RUNWARE_KEY in .env');

const runwareRetryAttempts = 8;
async function retryRunware(operation, label) {
  return retryTransient(operation, {
    attempts: runwareRetryAttempts,
    baseDelayMs: 3000,
    maxDelayMs: 30000,
    shouldRetry: (error) => isRetryableNetworkError(error) || isRetryableHttpStatus(error.status),
    onRetry: ({ nextAttempt, delay, error }) => {
      console.warn(`${label} retry ${nextAttempt}/${runwareRetryAttempts} in ${delay}ms · ${error.message}`);
    },
  });
}

const runDirectory = resolve(options.run);
const outputDirectory = resolve(options['output-dir'] || join(runDirectory, 'glue-vids'));
const ffmpeg = process.env.FFMPEG_BIN || 'ffmpeg';
const dataUrl = async (path) => `data:image/jpeg;base64,${(await readFile(path)).toString('base64')}`;
const json = async (path) => JSON.parse(await readFile(path, 'utf8'));
const exists = async (path) => {
  try { await stat(path); return true; } catch (error) { if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return false; throw error; }
};
const save = async (path, value) => {
  const temporary = `${path}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  await writeFile(temporary, `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`);
  await rename(temporary, path);
};
const pad = (value) => String(value).padStart(3, '0');

function runFfmpeg(args) {
  try {
    execFileSync(ffmpeg, args, { stdio: 'inherit' });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') throw new Error(`FFmpeg not found: ${ffmpeg}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

async function getIterations() {
  const runManifest = await json(join(runDirectory, 'run.json'));
  const iterations = runManifest.iterations?.map((entry) => entry.directory).filter(Boolean) || [];
  if (iterations.length < iterationLimit) throw new Error(`Run has ${iterations.length} completed iterations; requested ${iterationLimit}`);
  return iterations.slice(0, iterationLimit);
}

async function extractFrame(videoPath, outputPath, offsetFromStart, offsetFromEnd = false) {
  if (await exists(outputPath)) return;
  await mkdir(dirname(outputPath), { recursive: true });
  const args = ['-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error', '-y'];
  if (offsetFromEnd) args.push('-sseof', `-${offsetFromStart}`);
  else args.push('-ss', String(offsetFromStart));
  args.push('-i', videoPath);
  args.push('-frames:v', '1', '-q:v', '2', outputPath);
  runFfmpeg(args);
}

async function prepareTransition(iterationDirectory, previousScene, nextScene, transitionDuration, patternIndex) {
  const iterationNumber = Number(iterationDirectory.match(/iteration-(\d+)/)?.[1]);
  const previousNumber = Number(previousScene.index);
  const nextNumber = Number(nextScene.index);
  if (!Number.isInteger(iterationNumber) || nextNumber !== previousNumber + 1) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid scene boundary: ${iterationDirectory} ${previousScene.index} -> ${nextScene.index}`);
  }

  const iterationId = `iteration-${pad(iterationNumber)}`;
  const directory = join(outputDirectory, iterationId);
  const transitionId = `scene-${pad(previousNumber)}-to-${pad(nextNumber)}`;
  const previousVideo = join(runDirectory, iterationDirectory, 'media', `scene-${previousNumber}-video.mp4`);
  const nextVideo = join(runDirectory, iterationDirectory, 'media', `scene-${nextNumber}-video.mp4`);
  const firstFramePath = join(directory, `${transitionId}-first.jpg`);
  const lastFramePath = join(directory, `${transitionId}-last.jpg`);
  await extractFrame(previousVideo, firstFramePath, frameOffsetSeconds, true);
  await extractFrame(nextVideo, lastFramePath, frameOffsetSeconds);

  const before = previousScene.storyBeat || previousScene.title || '';
  const after = nextScene.storyBeat || nextScene.title || '';
  const articleTitle = (await json(join(runDirectory, iterationDirectory, 'record.json'))).article.title;
  const prompt = [
    `Create a single continuous ${transitionDuration}-second bridge shot for the article story "${articleTitle}".`,
    `The supplied first frame is from ${frameOffsetSeconds} seconds before the previous scene ends. The supplied last frame is from ${frameOffsetSeconds} seconds into the next scene.`,
    'Begin on the first frame and finish on the last frame. Let movement connect the two compositions naturally; no cut, fade, title, or new scene.',
    'Preserve the existing dark, nearly monochrome cubist mural style, charcoal, chalk-white and ash-gray palette, rough brushwork and shallow space.',
    'Keep the same people and visual motifs recognizable. Do not add readable text, logos, photorealism, or new characters.',
    `Previous scene: ${before}`,
    `Next scene: ${after}`,
    'Audio: quiet, non-verbal atmospheric sound; no speech and no music.',
  ].join('\n');

  const taskDirectory = directory;
  const requestPath = join(taskDirectory, `${transitionId}-request.json`);
  const pendingPath = join(taskDirectory, `${transitionId}-pending.json`);
  const submissionPath = join(taskDirectory, `${transitionId}-submission.json`);
  const responsePath = join(taskDirectory, `${transitionId}-response.json`);
  const videoPath = join(taskDirectory, `${transitionId}.mp4`);
  const savedRequest = await exists(requestPath) ? await json(requestPath) : null;
  const taskUUID = savedRequest?.taskUUID || randomUUID();
  const task = {
    taskType: 'videoInference',
    taskUUID,
    model: options.model,
    positivePrompt: prompt,
    inputs: {
      frameImages: [
        { image: await dataUrl(firstFramePath), frame: 'first' },
        { image: await dataUrl(lastFramePath), frame: 'last' },
      ],
    },
    resolution: options.resolution,
    duration: transitionDuration,
    numberResults: 1,
    deliveryMethod: 'async',
    includeCost: true,
    settings: { mode: options['settings-mode'] },
  };

  if (!savedRequest) await save(requestPath, {
    ...task,
    taskUUID,
    durationPatternIndex: patternIndex,
    frameOffsetSeconds,
    renderStartedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    inputs: { frameImages: [
      { image: firstFramePath, frame: 'first' },
      { image: lastFramePath, frame: 'last' },
    ] },
  });

  return {
    task, taskUUID, requestPath, pendingPath, submissionPath, responsePath, videoPath,
    firstFramePath, lastFramePath, transitionDuration, patternIndex,
  };
}

async function apiRequest(body, timeoutMs = 240_000) {
  const response = await fetch('https://api.runware.ai/v1', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
  });
  const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = new Error(`Runware HTTP ${response.status}: ${JSON.stringify(result).slice(0, 1200)}`);
    error.status = response.status;
    throw error;
  }
  return result;
}

async function getTaskDetails(taskUUID) {
  const response = await retryRunware(
    () => apiRequest([{ taskType: 'getTaskDetails', taskUUID }], 60_000),
    `Task lookup ${taskUUID}`,
  );
  const task = response.data?.find((item) => item.taskUUID === taskUUID);
  if (task) return task.response || null;
  if (response.errors?.some((error) => error.code === 'taskNotFound')) return null;
  if (response.errors?.length) throw new Error(`Runware task lookup failed: ${JSON.stringify(response.errors).slice(0, 1000)}`);
  return null;
}

async function submitOrRecover(job) {
  if (await exists(job.submissionPath)) return json(job.submissionPath);

  if (await exists(job.pendingPath)) {
    const recovered = await getTaskDetails(job.taskUUID);
    if (recovered) {
      await save(job.submissionPath, recovered);
      return recovered;
    }
  } else {
    await writeFile(job.pendingPath, `${JSON.stringify({ taskUUID: job.taskUUID, startedAt: new Date().toISOString() })}\n`, { flag: 'wx' });
  }

  try {
    const submission = await retryRunware(() => apiRequest([job.task]), `Submit ${job.taskUUID}`);
    await save(job.submissionPath, submission);
    return submission;
  } catch (error) {
    if (isRetryableNetworkError(error) || isRetryableHttpStatus(error.status)) {
      const recovered = await getTaskDetails(job.taskUUID);
      if (recovered) {
        await save(job.submissionPath, recovered);
        return recovered;
      }
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

async function getResult(job, submission) {
  const immediate = submission.data?.find((item) => item.taskUUID === job.taskUUID) || submission;
  if (immediate.videoURL) return immediate;

  const startedAt = Date.now();
  const timeoutMs = pollTimeoutSeconds * 1000;
  while (Date.now() - startedAt < timeoutMs) {
    await new Promise((resolvePromise) => setTimeout(resolvePromise, 2000));
    const remainingMs = timeoutMs - (Date.now() - startedAt);
    if (remainingMs <= 0) break;
    const response = await retryRunware(
      () => apiRequest([{ taskType: 'getResponse', taskUUID: job.taskUUID }], remainingMs),
      `Poll ${job.taskUUID}`,
    );
    if (response.errors?.length) throw new Error(`Runware poll failed: ${JSON.stringify(response.errors).slice(0, 1200)}`);
    const result = response.data?.find((item) => item.taskUUID === job.taskUUID);
    if (result?.videoURL) return result;
    if (result?.status && result.status !== 'processing') {
      throw new Error(`Runware ${job.taskUUID} failed: ${JSON.stringify(result).slice(0, 1200)}`);
    }
  }

  const error = new Error(`Runware ${job.taskUUID} still processing; rerun the command to resume without resubmitting`);
  error.code = 'VIDEO_STILL_PROCESSING';
  throw error;
}

async function downloadVideo(url, outputPath) {
  const bytes = await retryRunware(async () => {
    const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(120_000) });
    if (!response.ok) {
      const error = new Error(`Video download failed with HTTP ${response.status}`);
      error.status = response.status;
      throw error;
    }
    return Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer());
  }, 'Video download');
  await writeFile(outputPath, bytes);
}

async function processJob(job) {
  if (await exists(job.videoPath)) {
    console.log(`GLUE_EXISTS ${job.videoPath}`);
    return;
  }

  const savedResponse = await exists(job.responsePath) ? await json(job.responsePath) : null;
  let result = savedResponse;
  if (!result?.videoURL) {
    const submission = await submitOrRecover(job);
    if (submission.errors?.length || submission.error) throw new Error(`Runware submit failed: ${JSON.stringify(submission.errors || submission.error).slice(0, 1200)}`);
    result = await getResult(job, submission);
    await save(job.responsePath, result);
  }

  await downloadVideo(result.videoURL, job.videoPath);
  await save(job.responsePath, {
    ...result,
    downloadedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    localPath: job.videoPath,
    firstFrame: job.firstFramePath,
    lastFrame: job.lastFramePath,
  });
  if (await exists(job.pendingPath)) await unlink(job.pendingPath);
  console.log(`GLUE_READY ${job.videoPath} · $${Number(result.cost || 0).toFixed(4)}`);
}

const iterations = await getIterations();
const jobs = [];
const iterationRecords = [];
for (const iterationDirectory of iterations) {
  const iterationRecord = await json(join(runDirectory, iterationDirectory, 'record.json'));
  const scenes = iterationRecord.plan?.scenes || [];
  if (scenes.length < 2 && !isNewAutoSingleScene(iterationRecord)) {
    throw new Error(`${iterationDirectory} has fewer than two planned scenes`);
  }
  iterationRecords.push({ iterationDirectory, iterationRecord, scenes });
}
const hasTransitionCandidates = iterationRecords.some(({ scenes }) => scenes.length > 1);
const transitionWaitScheduler = transitionWaitPattern !== null && hasTransitionCandidates
  ? createTransitionWaitScheduler(transitionWaitPattern)
  : null;
for (const { iterationDirectory, scenes, iterationRecord } of iterationRecords) {
  const scheduledBoundaries = transitionWaitPattern !== null && scenes.length > 1
    ? transitionWaitScheduler.scheduleForIteration(scenes)
    : transitionWaitPattern !== null ? new Set() : null;
  for (let index = 0; index < scenes.length - 1; index += 1) {
    const boundaryKey = `${scenes[index].index}->${scenes[index + 1].index}`;
    if (scheduledBoundaries && !scheduledBoundaries.has(boundaryKey)) continue;
    const nextDuration = durationTaktmuster?.getNext();
    const transitionDuration = nextDuration?.patternValue ?? duration;
    jobs.push(await prepareTransition(
      iterationDirectory,
      scenes[index],
      scenes[index + 1],
      transitionDuration,
      nextDuration?.patternIndex ?? jobs.length,
    ));
  }
}

await mkdir(outputDirectory, { recursive: true });
const failures = [];
let nextJob = 0;
async function worker() {
  while (nextJob < jobs.length) {
    const job = jobs[nextJob];
    nextJob += 1;
    try { await processJob(job); } catch (error) {
      failures.push({ path: job.videoPath, message: error.message });
      console.error(`GLUE_FAILED ${job.videoPath}: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }
}

await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, jobs.length) }, () => worker()));
const summary = {
  runDirectory,
  outputDirectory,
  model: options.model,
  resolution: options.resolution,
  settingsMode: options['settings-mode'],
  requestedDurationSeconds: durationPattern ? null : duration,
  durationPatternSeconds: durationPattern,
  durationPatternType: durationPattern ? 'long cyclic getNext sequence' : null,
  durationPatternStepsConsumed: durationTaktmuster?.index ?? 0,
  transitionWaitPattern: transitionWaitPattern ?? 'Taktmuster 2/4/4 classic',
  transitionWaitPatternType: transitionWaitPattern === null
    ? null
    : 'Taktmuster.getNext().patternValue; persistent across iterations',
  transitionWaitPatternStepsConsumed: transitionWaitScheduler?.boundariesScheduled ?? 0,
  frameOffsetSeconds,
  iterationCount: iterations.length,
  transitionCount: jobs.length,
  transitionsPerIteration: jobs.length / iterations.length,
  completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  actualVideoCostUsd: (await Promise.all(jobs.map(async (job) => {
    if (!await exists(job.responsePath)) return 0;
    const response = await json(job.responsePath);
    return Number(response.cost || 0);
  }))).reduce((total, cost) => total + cost, 0),
  failures,
};
await save(join(outputDirectory, 'glue-run.json'), summary);
if (failures.length) throw new Error(`${failures.length} transition(s) failed; inspect glue-run.json and rerun to resume`);
